### person.educator_deduction — This person's educator expense deduction
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"const": 0}], "test": "le"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"input": "person.educator_expenses"}], "test": "lt"}, "then": {"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"of": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"param": "s1.educator_max"}], "op": "smaller_of"}}], "op": "choose"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Second:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"of": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"param": "s1.educator_max"}], "op": "smaller_of"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 93, Line 11: "If you were an eligible educator in 2025, you can deduct on line 11 up to $300 of qualified expenses you paid in 2025." Same formula as rounds 1 and 2 (helpers renamed): the smaller of the expenses and $300 for an eligible educator and zero otherwise. They differ only in which of the two dispositive questions is read first: the candidate-unknown records are interview records with zero expenses where the project rightly does not ask eligibility (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5, project $0.00), and the project-unknown records are partials withholding the expenses where the candidate's zero follows from eligibility false (classm.f8889.2.deepseek, classm.f4952.1.deepseek). No value differs. Stage 21 re-check: the reply's expression for this line and the project's line are unchanged from Stage 19 round 3 (the Stage 21 subject changes only the Form 1099-R totals and the Form 5329 block), and the new triage kinds are the same kinds over the larger sample (the Stage 21 fixtures added). Ruling: representation; i8889 p. 7, Line 6, Step 2: "Divide the refigured contribution limit from Step 1 equally between you and your spouse, unless you both agree on a different allocation". Same expression as round 2. The reply counts the person's Forms 8889 with f8889.allocation agreed without the spouse-rule filter. The allocation is asked only within the spouse rule, so the values supply it where the rule does not apply (classm.f8889.1 and classm.f8889.1.deepseek, both spouses, completed: project 0, candidate 1; generated 141 and 222 on refused returns), a record the interview cannot produce; household.hsa_agreed_count is read only on split forms. The candidate-unknown records are single-form returns without the allocation answer, which the interview does not ask there (stage11.hsa.self_full_year, stage11.hsa.family_55). Stage 21 re-check: the reply's expression for this line and the project's line are unchanged from Stage 19 round 3 (the Stage 21 subject changes only the Form 1099-R totals and the Form 5329 block), and the new triage kinds are the same kinds over the larger sample (the Stage 21 fixtures added). The value records now fall on refused returns (classm.f8889.6 and classm.f8889.6.deepseek, both spouses: project 0, candidate 1), the allocation answer supplied where the spouse rule does not apply, as ruled; the completed-return differences are the same single-form candidate-unknown records (stage11.hsa.self_full_year). The reply changed: it sums sc.s179_elected (lines 6 and 29 together) here and enters zero on person.s179_listed; its note says the supplied pages do not determine the split. That is so: the log gave i4562 pages 4 and 11 only, not page 5, which holds the line 6 and line 7 instructions, nor Form 4562; sc.f6 and sc.f29 were published by id and type only. Form 4562 line 8 (the sum of lines 6 and 7, s179.line8) is the same on both sides, so no deduction differs; the printed lines 6(c) and 7 do: stage14.asset.listed_and_not (completed, project $500.00, candidate $900.00), classm.eic.3 and classm.eic.3.deepseek (completed, listed property only: $0.00 against $10,918.00), stage14.veh.suv_179 ($0.00 against $31,300.00). The candidate refusals inherit vehicle screens the project applies to the same returns (scveh low_use, s179_bad, screened; classm.g28.2, stage14.veh.refuse_low_use; refused returns): scope on that kind. Stage 21 re-check: the reply's expression for this line and the project's line are unchanged from Stage 19 round 3 (the Stage 21 subject changes only the Form 1099-R totals and the Form 5329 block), and the new triage kinds are the same kinds over the larger sample (the Stage 21 fixtures added). Candidate refusals and unknowns are inherited placements of its own lines, ruled there: se_screen and rrta (classm.w2.1.deepseek), plan_screen (classm.dependent.7.deepseek), other forms' refusals reached through its modified AGI (classm.g28.2.deepseek scveh low_use), and ira_work_plan or plan asked where the interview does not ask them (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5). Project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (stage16.refuse.ira.both_kinds, stage16.refuse.ira.kbh_short; i1040gi p. 95, Line 20; p590a p. 35, excess contributions); project-unknown records are partials withholding sav_trad, where the candidate's zero follows from no contribution. Both copy this person's Schedule SE line 13; the differences are those of person.se_line13. The reply is again built on sc.line31 rather than sc.result; they differ only for a loss with amounts not at risk, which the project refuses at sc.loss_allowed (stage14.refuse.at_risk, the one project refusal; i1040sc p. 15, Line 32, "Be sure to attach Form 6198"). The candidate declares refusals on person.se_screen and person.rrta for every person; the project asks se_screen only of a person with a Schedule C, so the candidate-unknown records are interview records with no Schedule C where it is not asked (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5) and the candidate-refuses supply se_screen true for a person with no Schedule C (classm.f5695.2, classm.f5695.2.deepseek and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns, and the partial records built on them; classm.carloan.2, classm.f8959.1 and classm.w2.1.deepseek rrta on refused returns), an answer the interview does not produce for that person (scope on those kinds, as rounds 1 and 2). No value differs. Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit: "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)", and Student qualifications: "5. Filers and students must have been issued a TIN by the due date of their 2025 return (including extensions)." i8863 p. 2: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student enrolled at or attending an eligible educational institution." The reply changed: the student-TIN test is now person.tin, but without person.itin_applied, which the project accepts (p970 p. 11: "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; ruled in round 1); by reading, a student whose ITIN was applied for by the due date and issued gets no credit from the candidate (no such record in the sample). Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit: "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)", and Student qualifications: "5. Filers and students must have been issued a TIN by the due date of their 2025 return (including extensions)." i8863 p. 2: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student enrolled at or attending an eligible educational institution." The reply changed: the student-TIN test is now person.tin, but without person.itin_applied, which the project accepts (p970 p. 11: "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; ruled in round 1); by reading, a student whose ITIN was applied for by the due date and issued gets no credit from the candidate (no such record in the sample). It still omits f8863.paid, the return-level screen that precedes person.edu, so the candidate-unknown records are returns with f8863.paid no, where the interview never asks the student's answers (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5): its refusal declaration on person.edu_over is read first, an answer the source does not need when no expenses were paid for anyone (the quoted p. 2 sentence). Generated 47 (round 2's candidate-shows-skipped, the taxpayer with no TIN) now agrees. Its edu_over refusal matches the reworded question (BUG-0197). The worksheet arithmetic is the project's aqee definition. Lines 27 to 30 also omit the filers' TIN test (household.all_tin, amb.2025.edu_filer_tin, owner decision D7(a)); no record in the sample isolates it this round. Lines 28 to 30 carry no refusal declaration, so on stage19.refuse.period_over_person the project refuses at edu_over_refused and the candidate shows an amount on a return its own line 27 refuses: scope on that kind. Stage 21 re-check: the reply restructured its gates (a shared person.h_edu_base of person.edu, person.edu_1098t and person.tin, the worksheet line read directly from the aqee definition) with the same logic as round 3, so the ruling stands. ```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.employers
    printed: "employers"
    label: "Number of employers (Forms W-2) for this person — Schedule 3, line 11: had more than one employer for 2025"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one}
  - id: person.overcount
    printed: "overcount"
    label: "Number of employers that withheld more than $10,918.20 of social security tax"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, eq: [w2.over, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.over
    printed: "over"
    label: "If any one employer withheld more than $10,918.20, you can't claim the excess on your return"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.overcount, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.wages
    printed: "wages"
    label: "Total social security wages and tips (total of boxes 3 and 7 on Form(s) W-2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.wages}
  - id: person.withheld
    printed: "withheld"
    label: "Social security tax withheld (Form W-2, box 4), all employers"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box4}
  - id: person.eligible
    printed: "eligible"
    label: "More than one employer for 2025 and total wages of more than $176,100, and no one employer withheld more than $10,918.20"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [person.employers, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.over, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.wages, {param: ss.base}]}
  - id: person.excess
    printed: "excess"
    label: "Amount withheld in excess of $10,918.20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.withheld, take: {param: ss.limit}}
  - id: person.zero
    printed: "zero"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: person.credit
    printed: "credit"
    label: "Excess social security tax withheld (Schedule 3, line 11), figured separately for you and your spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.rrta]

  # ---- Schedule C / Schedule SE ----
  - id: person.sc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31 (all of this person's businesses)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}
  - id: person.h_se_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 3: combine lines 1a, 1b, and 2"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line3}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_se_line4a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 4a"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line4a}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 6: add lines 4c and 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line6}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment tax. Add lines 10 and 11 (Schedule SE line 12)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line12}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Deduction for one-half of self-employment tax (Schedule SE line 13; Schedule 1, line 15)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line13}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]

  # ---- SEP: Deduction Worksheet for Self-Employed (Pub. 560 ch. 5) ----
  - id: person.sep_s1
    printed: "Step 1"
    label: "Enter your net profit from Schedule C (Form 1040), line 31"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.sc_total}
  - id: person.sep_s2
    printed: "Step 2"
    label: "Enter your deduction for self-employment tax from Schedule 1 (Form 1040), line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.sep_s3
    printed: "Step 3"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment. Subtract step 2 from step 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s1, take: person.sep_s2}
    note: "The worksheet doesn't say what to do if the result is negative. I floored it at zero, since a negative amount can't support a contribution."
  - id: person.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}
  - id: person.h_hundred
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One hundred cents"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.hundred_cents}
  - id: person.h_sep_rate_c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan contribution rate (whole percent) expressed as cents"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_one_cent, count: person.sep_rate}
  - id: person.h_sep_denom
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Rate plus 1 (in hundredths)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_hundred, person.h_sep_rate_c]}
  - id: person.sep_s5
    printed: "Step 5"
    label: "Multiply step 3 by step 4 (rate from the Rate Table for Self-Employed)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.sep_s3, part: person.h_sep_rate_c, whole: person.h_sep_denom, places: 6}
    note: "Step 4 is the Rate Table entry, which equals rate ÷ (1 + rate) to six decimal places (for example 10% gives 0.090909). The table only covers whole percentages up to 25%; other rates are screened out by person.plan_screen."
  - id: person.h_comp_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$350,000 annual compensation limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.comp_limit}
  - id: person.sep_s6
    printed: "Step 6"
    label: "Multiply $350,000 by your plan contribution rate (not the reduced rate)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.h_comp_limit, part: person.h_sep_rate_c, whole: person.h_hundred, places: 2}
  - id: person.sep_s7
    printed: "Step 7"
    label: "Enter the smaller of step 5 or step 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s5, person.sep_s6]}
  - id: person.sep_s8
    printed: "Step 8"
    label: "Contribution dollar limit ($70,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.sep_dollar_limit}
  - id: person.sep_s21
    printed: "Step 21"
    label: "Maximum deductible contribution (no elective deferrals: the smaller of step 7 or step 8)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s7, person.sep_s8]}
  - id: person.h_sep_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Enter your actual contribution, not to exceed your maximum deductible contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}

  # ---- SIMPLE IRA (Pub. 560 ch. 3) ----
  - id: person.h_simple_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contribution limit ($16,500 for 2025)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.simple_limit}
  - id: person.h_simple_catchup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Catch-up contribution limit for your age"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: simple_catchup, keys: [person.simple_age]}
  - id: person.h_simple_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction limit plus catch-up limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_base, person.h_simple_catchup]}
  - id: person.h_simple_comp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation: net earnings from self-employment (line 4 of Schedule SE) before subtracting SIMPLE contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_se_line4a, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "\"Line 4\" could mean 4a or 4c. I used line 4a, floored at zero."
  - id: person.h_simple_elect_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, limited to the salary reduction and catch-up limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.h_simple_cap]}
  - id: person.h_simple_elective
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, not more than compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_elect_cap, person.h_simple_comp]}
  - id: person.h_simple_3pct
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp, rate: {param: ret.match_rate}}
  - id: person.h_simple_match_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Matching contribution limit: dollar-for-dollar up to 3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_elective, person.h_simple_3pct]}
  - id: person.h_simple_ne_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation taken into account for nonelective contributions (not more than $350,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_comp, person.h_comp_limit]}
  - id: person.h_simple_2pct
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "2% nonelective contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_ne_base, rate: {param: ret.nonelective_rate}}
  - id: person.h_simple_emp_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contribution limit for the plan's formula"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "match"}]}, then: person.h_simple_match_cap}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "nonelective"}]}, then: person.h_simple_2pct}
  - id: person.h_simple_emp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contributions made for yourself, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_employer, person.h_simple_emp_cap]}
  - id: person.h_simple_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE contributions for yourself (salary reduction plus employer contributions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_elective, person.h_simple_emp]}
  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SEP, SIMPLE, and qualified plans (this person's Schedule 1, line 16 amount)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}, then: person.zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}, then: person.h_sep_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_simple_ded}
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]

  # ---- Educator, interfaces ----
  - id: person.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: person.one}
  - id: person.educator_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expenses: up to $300 of qualified expenses paid in 2025 (each eligible educator)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_educator_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.h_educator_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$300 per eligible educator"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: s1.educator_max}
  - id: person.h_educator_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of qualified expenses or $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.educator_expenses, person.h_educator_max]}

  # ---- HSA (Form 8889 per person) ----
  - id: person.hsa_forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 8889 for this person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one}
  - id: person.hsa_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 8889 where spouses with separate HSAs had family coverage (line 6 split)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_agreed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 8889 using an agreed (not equal) allocation"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 Step 1: refigured family contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.step1}
  - id: person.hsa_share
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 Step 2: your allocated share"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.share}
  - id: person.hsa_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA deduction (Form 8889, line 13)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line13}
  - id: person.hsa_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions (Form 8889, line 16)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line16}
  - id: person.hsa_addtax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional 20% tax (Form 8889, line 17b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line17b}

  # ---- Tips / overtime ----
  - id: person.tip_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers paying qualified tips"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.one}
  - id: person.tip_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified Tips From More Than One Employer Worksheet line 2: add lines 1A through 1E, column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.col_d}
  - id: person.tip_reported
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified tips reported by employer on Form W-2 or reported to employer on Form 4070 (column (b) total)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.amount}
    note: "The pages don't define the name. I read it as the column (b) total, the amount used for line 4a."
  - id: person.ot_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers paying qualified overtime"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.one}
  - id: person.ot_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified Overtime From More Than One Employer Worksheet line 2: add lines 1A through 1E, column (b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.col_b}

  # ---- W-2 totals ----
  - id: person.box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}
  - id: person.box2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2, box 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box2}
  - id: person.medicare5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare wages and tips from Form W-2, box 5 (total)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box5}
  - id: person.medicare6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare tax withheld from Form W-2, box 6 (total)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box6}

  # ---- Form 1099-R totals ----
  - id: person.ira_amount_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA distributions (line 4a amounts)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.amount_a, where: {op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}}
  - id: person.ira_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA distributions, taxable amount (line 4b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.taxable, where: {op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}}
  - id: person.ira_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA distributions rolled over (line 4c box 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}, {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: person.plan_amount_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Pensions and annuities (line 5a amounts)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.amount_a, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}}
  - id: person.plan_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Pensions and annuities, taxable amount (line 5b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.taxable, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}}
  - id: person.plan_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Pension distributions rolled over (line 5c box 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}, {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: person.sav_dist
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880 line 4: 2025 distributions counted for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.sav_amount}

  # ---- Schedule C aggregates ----
  - id: person.sc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedules C"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one}
  - id: person.ho_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of businesses claiming business use of home (300 sq ft limit is for all businesses)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.ho_uses, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.mq_q4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of MACRS property placed in service during the last 3 months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_q4}
  - id: person.mq_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of MACRS property placed in service during the tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_total}
  - id: person.cost179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total cost of section 179 property placed in service (Form 4562, line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.cost179}
  - id: person.s179_nonlisted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elected cost of section 179 property (Form 4562, line 6, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.s179_elected}
    note: "The sc entry fields don't split the elected section 179 cost into line 6 (not listed) and line 7 (listed). This line therefore carries the whole elected cost, so the line 8 total (6 + 7) is still right."
  - id: person.s179_listed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Listed property elected section 179 cost (Form 4562, line 7)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.zero}
    note: "Can't be worked out from the supplied sc fields. The listed amount is included in person.s179_nonlisted so that line 8 is correct. This is a known limitation, not a finding that the amount is zero."
  - id: person.income179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Business income limitation: net income from Schedule C figured without the section 179 deduction"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31_pre179}
    note: "Covers Schedule C income only. Any wages for the line 11 limit are left to the s179 form."
  - id: person.positive_profits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7206 line 5: total of all net profits (don't include net losses)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.profit_pos}
  - id: person.sehi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed health insurance deduction (Form 7206, line 14)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f7206_line14}
  - id: person.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified business income or (loss) (Form 8995, line 1(c))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.qbi}
  - id: person.sc_1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Businesses with qualified tips or overtime for Schedule 1-A"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.tips_ot, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.eic_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Worksheet B line 1e: Schedule SE line 3 (plus 4b) minus Schedule SE line 13"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_se_line3, take: person.se_line13}

  # ---- Dependent care ----
  - id: person.dc_benefits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent care benefits (Form W-2, box 10), not including amounts reported as wages in box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.dc_benefits}
  - id: person.h_box10_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms W-2 with an amount in box 10"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, eq: [w2.has_box10, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.dc_has_box10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Received dependent care benefits in box 10"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_box10_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for the credit (Form 2441 lines 4/5): wages plus Schedule SE line 3 minus Schedule 1 line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.box1, person.eic_se]}
    note: "Line 1z includes line 1e (taxable benefits from Form 2441 line 26), which can't be attributed to a person here. That is left to f2441.attrib_gate."
  - id: person.dc_earned_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for lines 18/19 (doesn't include dependent care benefits on line 12)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.dc_earned}
    note: "Box 1 doesn't include the excluded box 10 benefits, so the two earned-income amounts are the same here."

  # ---- Saver's credit (Form 8880) ----
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Born after January 1, 2008; claimed as a dependent on someone else's return; or a student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_sav_both
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional plus Roth IRA contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_trad, person.sav_roth]}
  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional and Roth IRA contributions, and ABLE account contributions by the designated beneficiary for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sav_both}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: false}]}, then: person.sav_roth}
    refuses: [person.sav_able]
  - id: person.h_w2_sav
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals shown in box 12 of Forms W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}
  - id: person.h_simple_elective_used
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SIMPLE salary reduction contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_simple_elective}
        - {guard: {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals to a 401(k) or other qualified employer plan, voluntary employee contributions, and 501(c)(18)(D) plan contributions for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_w2_sav, person.sav_voluntary, person.h_simple_elective_used]}
    refuses: [person.sav_excess]

  # ---- IRA Deduction Worksheet ----
  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 11: traditional IRA contributions made for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_trad}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess]
  - id: person.ira_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 1a/1b: covered by a retirement plan (at work or through self-employment SEP/SIMPLE)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
  - id: person.h_mfs_apart
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and lived apart from spouse all of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_246
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$246,000 for the person who wasn't covered by a plan"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}
  - id: person.h_ira_line2_status
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 2 amount for the filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}
  - id: person.ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 2: amount that applies to you ($89,000; $146,000; $246,000 for the person not covered on a joint return; $10,000 MFS lived with spouse)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_246
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}]}}
          then: person.h_ira_line2_status
    refuses: [person.h_mfs_apart]
    note: "The $89,000 figure for MFS filers who lived apart isn't available as a parameter on its own, so that case is refused. The rule that an MFS spouse is treated as covered when the other spouse is covered can't be seen from this person's entry."
  - id: person.ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6: subtract line 5 from line 2 (none deductible if line 5 isn't less than line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_line2, take: ira.line5}
  - id: person.h_ira_lim
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_lim50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000 if age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable amount ($7,000, or $8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_lim50}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_lim}
  - id: person.h_ira_joint_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly or QSS, column of a person covered by a plan"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_full_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}
  - id: person.h_ira_full_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}
  - id: person.h_ira_full_thr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 threshold for the full amount ($20,000, or $10,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_narrow}
  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 70%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 80%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 40%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}
  - id: person.h_ira_prod
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 6 by the percentage that applies to you"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p40
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p35
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p80
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p70
  - id: person.h_ira_round
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_prod, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}
  - id: person.h_ira_phase
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_round, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 7 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, ge: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_full_thr]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_full_thr]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_phase
  - id: person.h_ira_7_10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 12: the smallest of line 7, 10, or 11. This is the most you can deduct"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_7_10, person.ira_contrib]}
  - id: person.h_ira_10_11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_contrib]}
  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 1: subtract line 12 of the worksheet from the smaller of line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_10_11, take: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "File Form 8606: nondeductible contributions for 2025, or distributions with basis more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_basis, {const: 0}]}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 2: enter your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]
  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distribution from a traditional IRA or Roth conversion in 2025 with nondeductible contributions in 2025 or an earlier year (Form 8606 lines 4–13)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [person.ira_basis, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line14
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 14 (no distribution: enter the amount from line 3, lines 1 plus 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}
    refuses: [person.ira_dist_gate]
  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction not limited by coverage: smallest of $7,000/$8,000, line 10, or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_lim_comp, person.ira_contrib]}
    note: "The pages don't define the name. I read it as the IRA deduction when no retirement-plan coverage limit applies. That is the only case where the SSB worksheet is used, because ssb.pub915 screens out the covered case."
  - id: person.h_ira_lim_comp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of the applicable amount or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_limit, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 9: net earnings from self-employment minus deductions on Schedule 1, lines 15 and 16. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.eic_se, take: person.s16}

  # ---- Form 8863 refundable AOC test and Part III ----
  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8863 line 7 conditions: under 18; or 18 or full-time student under 24 with earned income less than half of support; a parent alive; not filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2002, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
    note: "Age bands come from the birth-date questions: not born before Jan 2, 2008 means under 18; born before Jan 2, 2008 but not before Jan 2, 2007 means age 18; born before Jan 2, 2007 but not before Jan 2, 2002 means ages 19 to 23."
  - id: person.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments: tax-free educational assistance (2025 and 2026) and refunds"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a2025, person.edu_a2026, person.edu_refunds]}
  - id: person.h_edu_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified expenses paid, Form 1098-T requirement met, TIN issued"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit for this student (lines 23–26 lead to lines 27–30; EIN provided)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit for this student (go to line 31)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses. Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.edu_over]

  # ---- Form 5329 per-person aggregates ----
  - id: person.x_l1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329 line 1: early distributions includible in income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l1}
  - id: person.h_l2_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception amounts before per-person limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2}
  - id: person.h_cap09_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000 first-home limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_first_home}
  - id: person.h_cap09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Up to $10,000 reduced by all prior qualified first-time homebuyer distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_cap09_base, take: person.x_prior09}
  - id: person.h_e09_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "First-home exception (09) amounts"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e09}
  - id: person.h_e09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "First-home exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_e09_raw, person.h_cap09]}
  - id: person.h_cap19_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000 per birth or adoption"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_birth}
  - id: person.h_cap19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000 times the number of births or adoptions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_cap19_base, count: person.x_births}
  - id: person.h_e19_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Birth or adoption exception (19) amounts"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e19}
  - id: person.h_e19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Birth or adoption exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_e19_raw, person.h_cap19]}
  - id: person.h_cap22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,300 domestic abuse limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_abuse}
  - id: person.h_e22_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Domestic abuse exception (22) amounts"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e22}
  - id: person.h_e22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Domestic abuse exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_e22_raw, person.h_cap22]}
    note: "The other half of the limit (50% of the IRA's present value) can't be applied from the supplied inputs."
  - id: person.h_cap23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,000 emergency expense limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_emergency}
  - id: person.h_e23_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Emergency personal expense exception (23) amounts"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e23}
  - id: person.h_e23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Emergency exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_e23_raw, person.h_cap23]}
  - id: person.h_e23_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of emergency personal expense distributions"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.one, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e23, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.x_two23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "More than one emergency personal expense distribution in the calendar year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_e23_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
    note: "Read as the one-per-calendar-year limit on emergency personal expense distributions."
  - id: person.h_capped_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limited exception amounts before limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_e09_raw, person.h_e19_raw, person.h_e22_raw, person.h_e23_raw]}
  - id: person.x_limited_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limited exception amounts after per-person limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_e09, person.h_e19, person.h_e22, person.h_e23]}
  - id: person.x_other_exc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception amounts not subject to a per-person limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_l2_sum, take: person.h_capped_raw}
  - id: person.x_l2_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329 line 2: early distributions included on line 1 that are not subject to the additional tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.x_other_exc, person.x_limited_sum]}
  - id: person.x_limit_binds
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A per-person exception limit reduces line 2"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_capped_raw, person.x_limited_sum]}
  - id: person.x_l1_s
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 1 amounts from a SIMPLE IRA within 2 years (code S)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l1_s}
  - id: person.x_l2_s
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 2 exception amounts on code S distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_s}
    note: "This is the per-entry amount. The pages don't say how the per-person limits are split between SIMPLE and other distributions."
  - id: person.x_c05
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medical expense exception (05) amounts"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e05}
  - id: person.x_hsa_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329 line 47: excess contributions to HSAs for 2025, including excess employer contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.excess_amt}
  - id: person.x_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional taxes from this person's Form 5329 (Schedule 2, line 8)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f5329, field: f5329.tax}
    refuses: [person.x_other]
  - id: person.x_must
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 5329 this person must file"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f5329, field: f5329.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f5329.must, {const: true}]}}
```
```
